### [1] [PointGT: Simultaneous Geometry and Texture Editing for Point-Based Representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03341)
*Yanshu Zhang,George Shramko,Pratul P. Srinivasan,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: PointGT是一种基于点的3D表示方法，结合可形变的点表达与学习的UV映射技术，支持对物体几何和外观（纹理）进行同步精细编辑，同时保持高渲染质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有重建和视图合成技术（如3D Gaussian Splatting）虽然能产生高质量逼真的体积表示，但难以编辑。特别是已有的纹理编辑方法不兼容几何编辑和形变操作。

Method: 将适合几何形变的点基表示与学习的UV映射技术相结合，利用UV映射实现高分辨率纹理编辑，同时保持点的可变形特性以支持几何编辑。

Result: PointGT支持对基于点的神经表示进行几何和纹理的细粒度同步编辑，并保持高渲染质量。

Conclusion: PointGT解决了3D表示中几何与外观难以同时编辑的难题，为基于点的神经表示的精细编辑提供了新方案。

Abstract: We present PointGT, a point-based 3D representation that enables simultaneous editing of object geometry and appearance. Existing reconstruction and view synthesis techniques produce volumetric 3D representations that are high-quality and photorealistic, but are difficult to edit. In particular, recent efforts to enable texture editing for 3D Gaussian Splatting representations are not compatible with geometry edits and deformations. Our method combines a point-based representation that is well-suited for geometry deformations with a learned UV mapping technique that enables high-resolution texture editing. We show that PointGT enables fine-grained editing of both geometry and texture in point-based neural representations with high rendering quality.

</details>


### [2] [IDSPACE: A Novel Document Generator for Reliable Evaluation of Digital Identity Verification Systems [Extended Technical Report]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03052)
*Lulu Xie,Yancheng Wang,Kanchan Chowdhury,Rolando Garcia,Yingzhen Yang,Jia Zou*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了IDSpace，一种用于合成身份文档生成与优化的框架。它提出模型引导的贝叶斯优化来调优生成参数，解耦用户指定的元数据与自动调优的控制参数，并支持模板、扫描和手机拍摄等多种文档类型。实验表明，IDSpace在评估一致性、训练准确率和SSIM相似度上均优于现有基线，同时发布了包含359,240张合成文档的新数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线服务中验证远程用户身份需要依赖信任机构，但身份文档的敏感性导致真实数据稀缺，使欺诈检测工具的评估和调优变得困难。先前相关工作虽已被广泛下载（超11,000次下载），但仍有三个方面的局限需要解决：仅凭少量目标域样本难以优化生成参数、低层级生成参数与用户需求的耦合约制了可用性、以及生成范围局限于模板图像。

Method: IDSpace框架在三个方向上扩展了先前工作：1) 提出模型引导的贝叶斯优化方法，在仅给定少量目标域样本的条件下，同时最大化视觉相似性和预测一致性来调优生成参数；2) 将用户指定的元数据（如人口统计数据、欺诈模式、采集设备）与自动调优的控制参数（如字体风格、噪声水平、图像质量）解耦，允许用户无需底层专业知识即可配置评估；3) 将生成能力从模板图像扩展到扫描件和手机拍摄文档。

Result: 实验结果显示，相比CycleGAN、扩散修复（diffusion inpainting）和非引导优化等基线方法，IDSpace仅使用少量真实样本即可将评估一致性提升15-45%，训练准确率最高提升9%，与目标域的SSIM相似度提升10%。此外还发布了包含359,240张高质量合成文档、覆盖十种欧洲ID类型的新数据集。

Conclusion: IDSpace通过模型引导的优化、参数解耦和更广泛的文档类型支持，有效提升了合成身份文档在欺诈检测评估中的可用性和一致性，解决了真实数据稀缺带来的验证难题，并为该领域提供了大规模公开数据集资源。

Abstract: As services move online, trust institutions such as banks, lenders, and governments must verify the identity of remote users. Fraud detection tools are widely available, but evaluating and fine-tuning them remains difficult because identity documents are sensitive and therefore scarce. Synthetic data generation offers a path forward, and demand is clear: our prior work in this area has been downloaded over $11{,}000$ times (aggregated from eight parts). We introduce IDSpace, extending this line of research in three directions. First, we propose model-guided Bayesian optimization, which tunes generation parameters to maximize both visual similarity and prediction consistency with target-domain models given only a few samples from a target domain. Second, we decouple user-specified metadata (demographics, fraud patterns, capture device) from automatically tuned control parameters (font styles, noise levels, image quality), allowing users to configure evaluations without low-level expertise. Third, we expand beyond template images to support scanned and mobile-captured documents. Experiments show IDSpace improves evaluation consistency by $15-45\%$ over baselines including CycleGAN, diffusion inpainting, and non-guided optimization, using only a few real samples, while improving training accuracy by up to $9\%$ and SSIM similarity with the target domain by $10\%$. We also released a new dataset consisting of $359{,}240$ high-quality synthetic documents across ten European ID types.

</details>


### [3] [P-CORE: Self-Supervised Surface Consistency for Point-Based Neural Editing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03349)
*Yanshu Zhang,Shichong Peng,Mehran Aghabozorgi,Alireza Moazeni,Ke Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种自监督方法，使基于点的神经表示能够适应大规模非刚性变形，无需地面真值多视角图像。核心思想是对点云施加随机变形，并约束变形前后的表面预测保持一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多视角重建已能实现高保真效果，但自由形式的非刚性形状编辑仍具挑战。基于点的表示因无固定连接不受初始拓扑限制而备受青睐，但该特性导致其在大型变形下容易出现空洞和表面不连续问题。

Method: 提出自监督框架：生成随机变形，确保变形点云与原始点云两者表面预测的一致性——即变形点云的表面预测应等于对原始点云表面预测施加相同的变形。方法融入基于注意力机制的点表示（不同于基于splatting的点表示，后者使用高斯核，而前者使用可学习的点间插值核）。这种可学习的插值核可以适应大变形而无需增删点。

Result: 在Neural Editor和Objaverse合成几何编辑基准上，零样本编辑性能优于现有基于点的方法，且显著减少伪影；在DTU和Mip-NeRF 360真实场景数据上展示了方法的有效性。

Conclusion: 该框架显著增强了基于点表示对大型变形的鲁棒性，在合成和真实场景编辑任务中均优于现有方法，为基于点的神经表示的灵活形状编辑提供了有效解决方案。

Abstract: Advances in neural rendering have enabled high-fidelity multi-view reconstruction of 3D scenes. However, free-form non-rigid shape editing remains a significant challenge. Point-based neural representations are highly desirable for multi-view reconstruction because they lack fixed connectivity, which does not constrain the learned surface topology to that of the initialization. Yet this same property causes point-based representations to struggle with holes and surface discontinuities under large deformations. To address this, we propose a novel self-supervised method to enable point-based representations to adapt to large deformations without requiring ground truth multi-view images of deformed geometry. The key idea is to generate random deformations and to ensure consistency in the predicted surface before and after deformation. In particular, the surface prediction from the deformed point cloud should be the same as the deformation applied to the surface prediction from the original point cloud. We incorporate our approach into attention-based point representations, which differ from splatting-based point representations in their use of a learned interpolation kernel between points as opposed to a Gaussian kernel around each point. This learned interpolation kernel can learn to adapt to large deformations, without requiring addition or removal of points. We show that our framework significantly enhances its robustness to large deformations. Experiments on synthetic geometry editing benchmarks (Neural Editor, Objaverse) demonstrate that our approach outperforms existing point-based methods in zero-shot editing and significantly reduces artifacts. Furthermore, qualitative results on the DTU and Mip-NeRF 360 datasets demonstrate our method's effectiveness on real-world scenes.

</details>


### [4] [Position: Unlabeled IS NOT Equal to No Human Supervision in Visual Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03077)
*Dong Lao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文是一篇立场论文，认为无标签数据并不等同于无人类监督，呼吁研究社区更明确地识别和披露视觉学习中的监督来源。作者指出，当前“无监督”这一统一术语已无法区分不同数据筛选方案和训练目标中隐含的多样化人类先验，建议建立标准化的披露实践以提高学术交流的清晰度和公平性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 近年来计算机视觉中的自监督/预训练方法虽然在大型无标签数据上学习表征，但不同方法在数据筛选和训练目标中嵌入了各不相同的人类先验。当前统一使用“无监督”这一总括术语已无法捕捉这些重要区别，导致不同假设下开展的研究难以公平比较。同时，自2021年以来CV旗舰会议上带有“无监督”字样的论文数量急剧下降，即使该领域仍在持续增长，这一术语模糊性阻碍了学术交流和方法论多样性的保持。

Method: 本文是一篇论证性/立场论文（position paper），通过观察和分析CV领域中“无监督”术语的使用趋势（2021年后相关论文标题数量下降），指出当前术语分类体系的不足。作者从概念层面论证：无标签≠无人类监督，并主张将数据筛选方案和训练目标中嵌入的人类先验作为一种监督来源来显式区分。文中为研究社区提出了具体建议，包括作者应披露数据选择和学

Result: 本文为主要提出论证和观点的立场论文，无实验数据。其核心论点在于：需要重新审视“无监督学习”这一术语的含义，推动社区在论文中标准化披露数据选择和学习目标中隐含的监督来源，以改善学术交流、确保公平的方法比较并保留该领域的方法论多样性。

Conclusion: 作者完全支持预训练作为现代计算机视觉的坚实基础，但倡导学术界在概念清晰度上做出集体努力：作者应披露数据选择和训练目标中的先验信息，明确学习流水线中各组件依赖于哪些假设。标准化的披露实践可以提升学术沟通效率、保证方法比较的公平性，并维护无监督学习领域的方法论多样性。

Abstract: This position paper argues that the absence of labels does not imply the absence of human supervision in visual learning, and urges the research community to identify sources of supervision more explicitly. Many recent methods in computer vision build upon representations learned from large-scale unlabeled data, and are therefore grouped under the same umbrella term ``unsupervised.'' However, different data curation schemes and training objectives embed substantially different human priors on which models rely, and we argue that one ``unsupervised'' umbrella term is no longer capturing these distinctions. This ambiguity makes it harder to compare unsupervised learning research conducted under different assumptions, coinciding with a sharp decline in papers titled with ``unsupervised'' in flagship computer vision conferences since 2021, despite continued growth of the field. While we fully embrace pre-training as a strong foundation for modern computer vision, we advocate for a community-level effort toward greater conceptual clarity: authors are encouraged to disclose priors in data selection and learning objectives, and to specify which components of a learning pipeline depend on which assumptions. Standardized disclosure practices can improve academic communication, ensure fairer comparisons, and preserve methodological diversity in unsupervised learning.

</details>


### [5] [TruncGradGS: Improved 3D Gaussian Splatting via Truncated Gradient Updates](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03534)
*Theo Morales,Nhat-Quynh Le-Pham,Robin Atkins,Binh-Son Hua*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种分段截断梯度公式来解决3D高斯溅射（3D Gaussian Splatting）优化中的梯度消失问题，该问题导致远离高斯原语的像素难以有效影响原语属性更新。方法在静态和动态场景中均一致性地提升了重建质量，并引入了一个新的合成3D动态场景基准数据集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D Gaussian Splatting已成为新视角合成的代表性场景表示方法，但其标准优化依赖梯度更新，存在梯度消失现象——远离高斯原语的像素梯度幅度过小，无法有效调整原语属性，导致场景重建效果欠优。因此需要改进优化过程中的梯度传播机制以提高鲁棒性。

Method: 提出一种分段截断（piecewise truncated）梯度公式来替代标准梯度更新，通过截断非局部（far-field）区域的梯度以增强远端像素对高斯原语属性（如位置、尺度、颜色等）的优化信号，从而缓解梯度消失并提升优化稳定性与对初始化（随机和COLMAP）的鲁棒性。该方法可推广到静态与动态Gaussian Splatting，并配套构建了一个基于合成3D场景的动态场景基准数据集。

Result: 在静态和动态设置的公开基准以及新提出的动态数据集上均展示了方法的有效性，一致性地优于标准3D Gaussian Splatting在随机初始化和COLMAP初始化下的表现。同时揭示了现有动态场景基准的局限性。

Conclusion: 分段截断梯度公式能有效解决3D Gaussian Splatting中的梯度消失问题，提升优化稳定性与重建质量，并且具备跨静态/动态场景的泛化能力，为动态Gaussian Splatting提供了更可靠的基准评估数据集。

Abstract: 3D Gaussian Splatting has become a de facto scene representation for novel view synthesis, yet robustly learning 3D Gaussian primitives from visual input remains challenging. Standard optimization relies on gradient-based updates, but a common issue is the gradient vanishing phenomenon: a pixel far from a Gaussian primitive often has diminishing gradient magnitudes to influence primitive attributes, resulting in suboptimal scene reconstruction. In this paper, we propose a method to address gradient vanishing with a piecewise truncated gradient formulation that improves the optimization stability and robustness to initializations. We show that our method consistently improves 3D Gaussian Splatting with random and COLMAP initializations while being generalizable across static and dynamic Gaussian Splatting. As a by-product, we also examine the limitations of current benchmarks for dynamic scenes, and introduce a novel dataset for benchmarking dynamic Gaussian Splatting using synthetic 3D scenes. We demonstrate the effectiveness of our method in both static and dynamic settings for the public benchmarks and our proposed dataset.

</details>


### [6] [Exemplar: Classical Priors Complement Frozen Features for Few-Shot Microscopy Segmentation at Native Resolution](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03080)
*Michal Průšek,Adam Novozámský,Filip Šroubek*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: Exemplar是一个新的小样本生物医学图像分割器，将冻结的DINOv3骨干网络与固定的经典原生分辨率滤波器组融合在一个轻量级头部中，仅从支持掩膜进行拟合。在11个生物医学数据集的基准面板上，融合方法达到0.782的IoU/中心线Dice，优于单独使用任一组件（经典滤波器0.693，冻结特征0.672），并在55项方法-数据集对比中领先54项。即使只有一个标注掩膜也能达到0.703，超过从零训练的nnU-Net（0.682）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 新生物医学数据集的图像分割通常要么需要基于大量标注训练领域特定模型，要么依赖推理时的基础模型引导。作者旨在开发一种仅需少量支持掩膜（few-shot）即可实现良好分割性能的方法，无需繁重的标注或训练开销。

Method: Exemplar将冻结的DINOv3自监督骨干网络特征与固定的经典原生分辨率滤波器响应库（classical native-resolution filter bank）通过一个轻量级分割头部进行融合，该头部仅从支持掩膜（support masks）拟合得出，无需训练骨干网络。

Result: 在11个生物医学数据集上，经典滤波器库单独得分为0.693，冻结特征单独得分0.672，二者融合达到0.782。在55个方法对比中Exemplar领先54个（其中52个在Holm校正后显著）。单掩膜条件下达0.703，优于同条件下从零训练的nnU-Net（0.682）；八掩膜时nnU-Net在均值上反超（主要在中心线一致性上），但拟合耗时长16-77倍。

Conclusion: 经典先验与冻结自监督特征在少样本原生分辨率分割中是互补的。Exemplar以单一固定配置在跨11个数据集的广泛分割任务上展现了强大的少样本性能，在极少量标注场景下具有实用价值。

Abstract: Segmenting a new biomedical dataset usually means a domain-specific model trained on substantial annotation, or a foundation model steered at inference time. We present Exemplar, a few-shot segmenter that fuses a frozen DINOv3 backbone with a fixed bank of classical native-resolution filter responses in one lightweight head, fitted from the support masks alone. In the few-mask, native-resolution regime, classical priors and frozen self-supervised features are complementary: fused in one head, a single fixed configuration spans eleven biomedical imaging datasets. Under the same head, the classical bank alone reaches 0.693 on the eleven-dataset panel, scored by foreground intersection-over-union or centreline Dice, and the frozen features alone 0.672; the bank leads on seven of the eleven and the features on the rest, and fused they reach 0.782. Against five forward-pass few-shot methods, Exemplar leads in 54 of 55 method-dataset comparisons, 52 of them significant after Holm correction. From a single annotated mask it reaches 0.703 on the same panel, against 0.682 for a from-scratch nnU-Net trained on that same mask. At eight masks nnU-Net overtakes it on the panel mean, chiefly on centreline agreement, but takes 16-77x longer to fit.

</details>


### [7] [Building Pretraining Data for World Models: An Unreal Engine-Based Pipeline for Action-Conditioned Video Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03557)
*Haoyu Wang,Songchun Zhang,Haoran Li,Haoyang Huang,Zeyue Xue,Nan Duan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一个基于Unreal Engine的大规模合成数据生产流水线，用于生成动作条件化、多视角视频数据。流水线采用两阶段设计：第一阶段在PIE中运行实时物理并记录角色状态与控制输入，第二阶段在新引擎进程中重放轨迹并通过Movie Render Queue进行离线高质量渲染。该流水线在25台服务器（各配8块RTX 5090）上运行，从2,384个资产包中筛选出429个关卡和40个人形角色，已产出2,691小时1080p视频和6,076小时720p视频。该方案构成EchoWM中的Unreal Engine合成数据生产组件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 动作条件化视频模型需要大规模视觉数据与时间对齐的控制信号配对，但真实世界视频中导致视觉变化的动作通常未知，难以获取此类监督信号。因此需要一种能够大规模合成动作条件化、多视角视频数据的生产方案。

Method: 提出两阶段生产流水线：Stage I在PIE中运行实时物理引擎，记录每帧角色状态、控制输入和相机状态至中间轨迹表示；Stage II在新引擎进程中重放轨迹并以Movie Render Queue进行离线高质量渲染。同时构建分布式生产系统，包含缓存感知的任务分区、节点本地槽位调度、自动场景筛选、美学与亮度过滤、部分输出恢复、异步上传以及持续集群健康监控。

Result: 生产集群含25台服务器共200块RTX 5090 GPU；从2,384个资产包中保留429个关卡与40个人形角色用于生产；产出2,691小时1080p视频和6,076小时720p视频。

Conclusion: 描述系统架构、从生产失败中得出的实现决策，以及使用感知质量代理进行世界模型数据策展的局限性。该流水线构成EchoWM的Unreal Engine合成数据生产组件。

Abstract: Action-conditioned video models require large-scale visual data paired with control signals that are temporally aligned with the resulting scene transitions. Such supervision is difficult to obtain from ordinary real-world video because the actions that caused each visual change are typically unknown. We present a large-scale synthetic data production pipeline built on Unreal Engine for generating action-conditioned, multi-view video. To accommodate the different execution requirements of real-time physics and high-quality offline rendering, the pipeline executes trajectory generation and final rendering in two stages: Stage I runs real physics in PIE and records per-frame character states, control inputs, and camera states into an intermediate trajectory representation; Stage II replays those trajectories in a new engine process and renders them offline with Movie Render Queue (MRQ). Around this core, we develop a distributed production system with cache-aware task partitioning, node-local slot scheduling, automated scene screening, aesthetic and luminance filtering, partial-output recovery, asynchronous upload, and continuous cluster health monitoring. The production cluster contains 25 servers with eight NVIDIA RTX 5090 GPUs per server. From 2,384 asset packs, 429 levels were retained for production together with a pool of 40 humanoid characters. The pipeline has produced 2,691 hours of 1080p video and 6,076 hours of 720p video. We describe the system architecture, the implementation decisions that emerged from production failures, and the limitations of using perceptual quality proxies for world-model data curation. The pipeline described in this report constitutes the Unreal Engine synthetic-data production component used in EchoWM.

</details>


### [8] [Solving the Needle-in-a-Haystack Problem in Mammography Vision-Language Model with Differentiable Subset Sampling](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03085)
*Young Seok Jeon,Beatrice Brown-Mulry,Rohan Satya Isaac,Anjana Dissanayaka,Theo Dapamede,Mohammadreza Chavoshi,Judy Gichoya,Hari Trivedi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出TopKSigLIP，一种专为乳腺X光影像设计的视觉-语言模型，通过TopK-Patch模块对高分辨率影像进行稀疏面片采样以解决分辨率-批大小权衡问题，并用Sup-sigmoid损失（软标签扩展的SigLIP sigmoid损失）替代对比损失以缓解报告同质性问题。该模型在密度评估、BI-RADS分类、发现亚型及癌症预测等零样本任务上超越现有开源乳腺及通用医学VLM，其TopK-Patch模块的病灶定位性能亦优于Grad-CAM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准CLIP架构与训练目标直接用于乳腺X光影像时，在癌症检测、发现类型和BI-RADS预测等临床重要任务的零样本性能有限。作者认为原因在于忽视了两个乳腺影像数据的特征：（1）高分辨率特性；（2）影像报告的高度同质性（主要由阴性/良性发现主导）。

Method: TopKSigLIP包含两点创新：（1）架构层面，提出TopK-Patch模块，学习从高分辨率乳腺X光影像中采样可能包含病灶的稀疏高分辨率面片集，规避下采样导致的GPU内存约束，同时跳出分辨率与批大小之间的权衡；采样位置本身还可作为内置的定位工具。（2）训练目标层面，用Sup-sigmoid损失替代对比损失，扩展SigLIP的sigmoid损失并引入源自结构化数据的软标签，避免对比损失错误地排斥语义相近的样本对。

Result: TopKSigLIP在内部与外部基准的零样本评估中，于密度评估、BI-RADS分类、发现亚型和癌症预测任务上均优于现有开源乳腺与通用医学VLM。在线性探测协议下尽管使用更小的视觉编码器和更小的训练批，仍保持竞争力。TopK-Patch模块的病灶定位性能优于事后Grad-CAM方法。

Conclusion: TopKSigLIP通过针对乳腺影像高分辨率和报告同质性两大特征设计的架构（TopK-Patch模块）与损失函数（Sup-sigmoid损失），有效提升了乳腺X光影像VLM的零样本临床任务性能，并提供内建的病灶定位能力，代码与权重已公开。

Abstract: There is growing interest in adopting CLIP-style vision--language model (VLM) pretraining for mammography. However, models that directly employ the standard CLIP architecture and training objective exhibit limited zero-shot performance in clinically important tasks such as cancer, finding-type, and BI-RADS predictions. We argue that this underwhelming performance is due to neglecting two characteristics of mammography data: (1) its high-res nature, and (2) homogeneity of radiology reports, largely driven by a predominance of negative/benign findings on examinations. We propose TopKSigLIP, a VLM designed to address these two limitations through a novel architecture and learning objectives. Instead of downscaling high-res mammography images to satisfy GPU memory constraints, TopKSigLIP introduces TopK-Patch module that learns to sample a sparse set of high-res patches likely to contain lesions, sidestepping the resolution--batch size tradeoff of VLM training. The sampled patch locations additionally serve as a built-in localization tool. To address report homogeneity, we replace the contrastive loss, which falsely repels semantically similar pairs, with a Sup-sigmoid loss. Sup-sigmoid loss extends the sigmoid loss from SigLIP with soft labels derived from structured data. TopKSigLIP outperforms existing open-source mammography and general medical VLMs on both internal and external benchmarks on density assessment, BI-RADS classification, finding subtyping, and cancer prediction under zero-shot evaluation. TopKSigLIP remains competitive under linear probing despite using a significantly smaller vision encoder and smaller training batches than baselines. The TopK-Patch module additionally achieves superior lesion localization over post-hoc Grad-CAM. Code and weights are made public:https://github.com/Youngseok0001/TopKSigLIP.

</details>


### [9] [WireSeg-32K: A Physics-Grounded Synthetic Dataset for Wire Instance Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03102)
*Zilin Dai,Lehong Wang,Yi Yang,Xiang Fei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出WireSeg-32k，一个用于线缆实例分割的大规模合成数据集（含32,000张RGB图像、实例掩码、深度图及真实世界测试集），并开发了DeformX协同仿真管线，将Cosserat杆动力学与Isaac Sim物理渲染耦合以生成物理真实、接触一致的线缆形变数据。基于LoRA微调的SAM3基线在真实场景mAP@75上提升了10.2%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 可变形线性物体（如线缆）由于细长、高度形变且频繁自遮挡的特性难以分割；真实场景下大规模实例级标注成本高昂；现有资源要么局限于受约束条件下的线缆跟踪或语义分割，要么生成仅有视觉合理性却缺乏物理根据的形变数据。

Method: 通过DeformX协同仿真管线，将Cosserat杆动力学与基于Isaac Sim的逼真渲染相结合，生成物理合理、接触一致的线缆形状；利用CAD线缆资产构建多样化的视觉接地场景；构建32K规模的合成数据集，并以LoRA微调SAM3作为基线验证。

Result: 仅使用WireSeg-32k对SAM3进行LoRA微调，在真实世界mAP@75上相比开箱即用模型提升了10.2%，证明了物理接地的合成数据可有效迁移至真实线缆感知任务。

Conclusion: 物理真实的合成数据在可变形线缆实例分割中有效，DeformX与WireSeg-32k为真实场景下的线缆感知提供了可扩展的数据方案，弥合了合成数据与真实世界之间的领域差距。

Abstract: Deformable linear objects such as wires and cables are difficult to segment because they are thin, highly deformable, and frequently self-occluded, while large-scale instance-level annotations are expensive to obtain in real scenes. Existing resources either focus on cable tracing or semantic segmentation under constrained settings, or generate visually plausible images without physically grounded wire deformation. We present WireSeg-32k, a synthetic dataset for wire instance segmentation with 32,000 RGB images, instance masks, depth maps, and a complementary real-world test set with annotations. To generate this dataset, we develop DeformX, a co-simulation pipeline that couples Cosserat-rod dynamics with photorealistic Isaac Sim rendering, enabling physically plausible, contact-consistent wire shapes, CAD-based wire assets, and diverse visually grounded scenes. As a simple baseline, LoRA fine-tuning SAM3 on WireSeg-32k alone improves real-world mAP@75 by 10.2% over the off-the-shelf model, showing that physically grounded synthetic data can transfer to real wire perception.

</details>


### [10] [SLIDEFORGE: An LLM Agent for Controllable Editing of Slides as Structured Artifacts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03109)
*Haozhen Zheng,Fulin Wang,Tianhu Xiong,Yingjie Yu,Shengyi Qian,Hanchao Yu,Alex Schwing,Klara Nahrstedt,Mingyuan Wu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SLIDEFORGE，一个用于可控幻灯片编辑的智能体框架，通过构建Deck State Graph（可执行的幻灯片状态图），链接视觉分解、原生pptx对象结构与感知组织，实现保留布局、风格、组件结构和原生可编辑性的幻灯片编辑，并引入了一个联合评估组件恢复、保真、重样式一致性、视觉质量和原生可编辑性的评估范式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的AI智能体虽然能很好地描述幻灯片内容，但在AI辅助幻灯片编辑任务中，输出必须保留布局、样式、组件结构和原生可编辑性。然而，现有智能体通常基于截图或弱文档表示进行操作，往往会导致连贯视觉单元被分割、可编辑内容被栅格化或布局被破坏。因此需要一种能够同时保持可编辑性、结构完整性和视觉连贯性的可控幻灯片编辑框架。

Method: SLIDEFORGE构建了Deck State Graph，这是一种可执行的幻灯片状态表示，将三个层面关联起来：视觉分解（visual decomposition）、原生pptx对象结构（native pptx object structure）和感知组织（perceptual organization）。通过恢复人类可指涉的组件同时保留细粒度的可编辑结构，SLIDEFORGE通过幻灯片原生操作和渲染状态验证来实现主题保留的重构。同时提出了一个新的评估范式，联合测量组件恢复、保真度、重样式一致性、视觉质量和原生可编辑性。

Result: 实验表明，SLIDEFORGE在组件恢复、保真度、重样式一致性、视觉质量和原生可编辑性等多个维度上均优于直接提示（direct prompting）、基于截图的智能体（screenshot-based agents）和通用代码智能体基线（generic code-agent baselines）。

Conclusion: SLIDEFORGE通过引入Deck State Graph这一可执行状态表示，有效弥合了AI智能体理解与可控原生编辑之间的鸿沟，为AI辅助幻灯片编辑提供了兼顾结构完整性、视觉质量与原生可编辑性的解决方案，并配套了系统的评估范式。

Abstract: Current AI agents compellingly describe slides. However, AI-assisted slide editing requires more than understanding: the output must retain layout, style, component structure, and native editability. Towards, AI-assisted slide editing, existing agents operate on screenshots or weak document representations and often fragment coherent visual units, rasterize editable content, or break layout. In contrast, for controllable slide editing, we introduce an agentic framework, SLIDEFORGE, which builds a Deck State Graph, an executable slide state that links visual decomposition, native pptx object structure, and perceptual organization. By recovering human-referable components while retaining fine-grained editable structure, SLIDEFORGE supports theme-preserving reconstruction through slide-native operations and rendered-state verification. We further introduce an evaluation paradigm for controllable slide transformation that jointly measures component recovery, preservation, restyling consistency, visual quality, and native editability. Experiments show that SLIDEFORGE outperforms direct prompting, screenshot-based agents, and generic code-agent baselines across these dimensions. Code is available at https://github.com/UIUC-MONET/SLIDEFORGE.

</details>


### [11] [Beyond Small Patches: Black-Box Detection and Purification of Diverse Backdoor Triggers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03139)
*Ahmed Abdelnaby,Mohamed Elmahallawy*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: TRIM是一种部署导向的黒箱后门防御方法，在推理阶段无需模型内部、训练数据或干净样本即可检测和选择性地移除后门触发器，通过区域分割、触发器发现和选择性区域净化三个关键组件，将攻击成功率降至最低1.16%同时保持干净准确率高达87.87%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 深度神经网络在实际视觉系统中易受后门攻击影响，现有防御方法往往依赖模型内部结构、训练数据或干净验证样本，难以在仅有黑盒访问权限的实际部署场景中使用。作者旨在提出一种只需黑盒访问、无需任何辅助数据的推理时后门防御方法。

Method: TRIM包含三个关键组件：(i) 基于深度特征表示的区域分割，识别图像中可疑区域；(ii) 通过图像修复和扩散重建的自适应触发器发现机制，隔离导致误分类的区域，无需假设触发器类型、形状或位置；(iii) 选择性区域净化，仅清洗被污染区域而保留良性内容。此外，TRIM缓存先前识别触发器的特征嵌入，可高效识别并避免重复检测和净化。

Result: 在多种数据集和多种后门类型（包括混合型、稀疏型、变尺寸和多触发器）的实验中，TRIM一致优于现有黑盒防御方法，将攻击成功率降至最低1.16%，同时保持干净准确率高达87.87%。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明即使在防御方无法访问任何辅助数据的推理阶段，也能实现有效的后门缓解，为实际部署场景中的黑盒后门防御提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Deep neural networks (DNNs) are increasingly deployed in real-world vision systems, yet their predictions can be covertly manipulated by backdoor attacks, in which malicious triggers cause targeted misclassification while preserving high clean accuracy. Existing defenses often rely on model internals, training data, or clean validation samples, making them difficult to deploy when only black-box access to a trained model is available. We propose TRIM (Trigger Removal by Identifying Manipulated Regions), a deployment-oriented black-box defense that detects and selectively removes backdoor triggers at inference time without requiring model internals, training data, or clean samples. The key insight behind TRIM is to identify image regions that are responsible for anomalous model behavior and purify only those regions while preserving benign content. TRIM innovates via three key components: (i) region-based segmentation with deep feature representations, (ii) adaptive trigger discovery through inpainting and diffusion-based reconstruction to isolate regions responsible for misclassification---without assumptions about trigger type, shape, or location, and (iii) selective region purification that cleans poisoned regions while retaining benign content. To support practical deployment, TRIM further caches feature embeddings of previously identified triggers, enabling efficient recognition and avoiding redundant detection and purification. Extensive experiments across diverse datasets and backdoor types, including blended, sparse, varying-size, and multiple triggers, show that TRIM consistently outperforms existing black-box defenses, reducing attack success rates (ASR) to as low as 1.16% while preserving clean accuracy of up to 87.87%. These results demonstrate that effective backdoor mitigation is possible at inference time even when the defender has no access to any auxiliary data.

</details>


### [12] [VeriPhy: Agentic Physical Reasoning for World Model Evaluation and Refinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03153)
*Wenzhuo Xu,Yuchen Zhu,Chongjian Ge,Xuan Shen,Jing Shi,Jason Kuen,Yongxin Chen,Molei Tao,Christopher McComb,Noelia Grande Gutiérrez,Jiuxiang Gu*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: VeriPhy是一个可审计的物理验证系统，用于视频生成结果的物理可靠性检查。它通过文本规划器将提示编译为类型化物理责任和执行计划，在执行过程中将观测限制在仅声明的冻结低层专家调用上，每个动作返回携带溯源证据的记录，并通过类型化解析器映射为三态判定（支持/矛盾/未知），每个判定都可追溯到产生它的证据。在1,500片段语料和149片段核心集上，VeriPhy正确解释了228条缺陷记录（对比问题分解评估器的164条），且每个决策保留证据记录和溯源，支持逐判定审计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视频生成质量评估方法无法区分视觉流畅性与物理可靠性——一个标量质量分数无法指示视频片段违反的物理义务类型或失败的精确时刻，缺乏可审计、可追溯的物理验证能力。

Method: VeriPhy构建了一个流水线：(1) 文本only规划器在观察任何帧之前将提示编译为类型化物理义务和静态验证的执行计划；(2) 执行期间观测仅门户化并限定调用冻结的低层专家（包括分割跟踪、计数、11种物理测量、深度、OCR、音频事件检测）；(3) 每个动作返回携带溯源（provenance）的证据记录，其有效载荷是类型化测量值或明确标记的学习状态；(4) 类型化解析器和固定组合规则将可用记录映射为三值状态（支持/矛盾/未知），并携带完整溯源，使每个判定可追溯到产生它的证据。

Result: 在包含1,500个带人工标注缺陷记录的片段语料上进行锚定评估，在包含304条缺陷记录的149片段核心集上，VeriPhy解释了228条，而已发表的问题分解评估器仅为164条。虽然单纯回忆率上与同一骨干网络的整体提示方法（222条）相差不大，但关键差异在于VeriPhy的每个决策都保留证据记录和溯源，使追踪可逐判定审计，并可作为评论者判定写回生成过程的接口。

Conclusion: VeriPhy的核心贡献不在于显著提高物理缺陷的召回率，而在于引入了可审计的物理验证架构——每个验证判定都附带完整的证据链和溯源信息，使系统不仅能够报告'是否合理'，还能精确说明'为什么'以及'何时'失败，为生成系统提供了可写回的判定接口。

Abstract: Visual fluency in generated video does not imply physical reliability, and a scalar quality score alone is incapable of indicating the obligation a clip violates or the moment it fails. We present VeriPhy, an auditable physical-verification system in which a text-only planner compiles the prompt into typed physical obligations and a statically validated execution plan before any frame is observed. During execution, observations gate and scope only declared calls to frozen low-level experts (e.g., segmentation and tracking, counting, eleven typed physical measurements over the resulting tracks, depth, OCR, and audio-event detection). Each action returns a provenance-carrying evidence record whose payload, when usable, is either a typed measurement or an explicitly tagged learned state. Typed resolvers and fixed composition map usable records to a three-valued state (supported, contradicted, or unknown, surfaced as plausible, implausible, or abstain) with full provenance, so that every verdict is traceable to the evidence that produced it. We anchor evaluation in a 1,500-clip corpus of human-annotated flaw records that localize real generation failures in prompt reference, space, and time. On a 149-clip core carrying 304 such records, VeriPhy accounts for 228, against 164 for a published question-decomposition evaluator given the same clips and the same claims. Recall alone does not separate it from prompting the same backbone monolithically, which reaches 222; what separates them is that each decision retains its evidence record and provenance, making the traces auditable one verdict at a time and usable as the interface through which a critic verdict could be written back into generation.

</details>


### [13] [Who Speaks for the Pruned? Visual Token Pruning as Coverage Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03158)
*Qingchan Zhu,Weihang You,Hanqi Jiang,Changdi Yang,Tianming Liu,Geng Yuan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: CoverPruner是一种无需训练的视觉token剪枝方法，通过"代表性覆盖最大化"(RCM)来回答"token被移除后由哪个幸存token代表它"这一需求侧问题，在多VLM架构和压缩率下取得了最佳平均准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有视觉token剪枝方法多只关注"保留哪些token"，即保留侧视角，这种方式可能保留冗余的高分token，同时丢弃了那些没有近邻代表的有效证据。作者提出应从需求侧重新审视剪枝问题：被移除的token是否被某个幸存token充分代表。

Method: CoverPruner将剪枝形式化为表征覆盖最大化(RCM)问题，即用查询(query)加权的需求覆盖完整投影后的视觉token集合。具体通过投影器空间覆盖(projector-space coverage)和轻量级的第一层注意力探针(first-layer attention probe)来实例化RCM，且无需训练。

Result: 在多种VLM架构和不同压缩率下，CoverPruner在所有对比方法中取得了最佳平均准确率，且最大的性能提升通常出现在激进压缩（高剪枝率）的场景下。

Conclusion: 从保留侧转向覆盖/需求侧的视角来设计token剪枝策略是有效的，CoverPruner通过覆盖最大化框架在无需训练的情况下提升了VLM推理压缩的效率与准确性。

Abstract: Visual token pruning reduces the inference cost of vision-language models (VLMs), but most methods only ask which tokens to keep. This retained-token view can keep redundant high-scoring tokens while leaving discarded evidence without a close representative. We propose CoverPruner, a training-free pruner that asks the complementary demand-side question: after a token is removed, which surviving original token represents it for the target VLM? CoverPruner formulates pruning as Representational Coverage Maximization (RCM), covering the full projected visual-token set with query-weighted demand. It instantiates RCM with projector-space coverage and a lightweight first-layer attention probe. Across multiple VLM architectures and compression rates, CoverPruner achieves the best average accuracy among all compared methods, with the largest gains usually appearing under aggressive compression.

</details>


### [14] [RoboTok: An Internet-Scale Data Engine for Human Demonstration Retrieval and Dexterous Manipulation Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03199)
*Howard Qian,Yiting Chen,Yunfei Xie,Kejia Ren,Podshara Chanrungmaneekul,Gaotian Wang,Bowen Wen,Chen Wei,Kaiyu Hang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: RoboTok是一个互联网规模的数据引擎，通过从网络视频中检索与查询人类操作视频相关的演示数据，用于训练灵巧机器人策略。该方法学习了一个基于3D手部轨迹的潜在运动空间，能够跨越视角、场景外观和遮挡差异比较操作行为，从而提升检索相关性和下游任务成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人学习依赖广泛多样的演示数据，但收集机器人数据成本高昂，难以覆盖真实世界任务的长尾分布，亟需一种能从互联网海量视频中自动获取可训练数据的方法。

Method: RoboTok学习一个潜在运动空间，基于以估计的参与者中心参考系表示的3D手部轨迹。该表示支持操作行为的跨视角、场景外观和参与者遮挡比较，同时保持紧凑以支持互联网规模视频集合的高效搜索和持续索引更新。

Result: 在与现有机器人数据检索方法的对比中，RoboTok在检索基准和下游机器人策略性能上均表现更优，检索到了更相关的操作演示并提升了下游任务成功率。

Conclusion: 实验结果证实了手部姿态轨迹感知的检索方式是可行的，建立了一种将网络视频作为机器人学习可扩展且持续增长监督源的范式。

Abstract: Robot learning increasingly depends on broad and diverse demonstrations, yet collecting robot data remains expensive and poorly suited to covering the long tail of real-world tasks. To address this bottleneck, we introduce RoboTok, an internet-scale data engine that, given a query human manipulation video, retrieves manipulation-relevant human demonstrations from web videos for training dexterous robot policies. Specifically, we learn a latent motion space from 3D hand trajectories expressed in estimated actor-centered reference frames. This representation enables manipulation behaviors to be compared across variations in camera viewpoint, scene appearance, and actor occlusions, while remaining compact enough for efficient search and continual indexing over internet-scale video collections. We evaluate RoboTok against existing robot-data retrieval approaches on retrieval benchmarks and downstream robot policy performance. Our results show that RoboTok retrieves more relevant manipulation demonstrations and improves downstream task success, establishing hand-pose trajectory-aware retrieval as a way to make web video a scalable and continuously growing source of supervision for robot learning.

</details>


### [15] [Learning to Zoom Efficiently with a Contrastive Curriculum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03206)
*Falko Helm,Iryna Gurevych*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需额外标注或预热监督微调的InfoNCE风格内在奖励方法，用于教会多模态大语言模型使用缩放工具处理高分辨率图像。通过课程化递进式负样本对比训练，在V*、HRBench和MME-RealWorld基准上取得与SFT相当或更优的性能；同时引入可扩展的合成Muffin&Chihuahua(M&C)数据集，直接衡量模型的缩放能力，发现召回率是最终任务性能的最强关联指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的视觉智能体学习缩放工具使用需要大量监督微调(SFT)预热阶段，这不仅需要额外的标注成本，也限制了训练效率。作者旨在探索无需标签或SFT即可教会多模态大语言模型(MLLM)使用缩放工具的方法，并建立直接评估缩放能力的标准化数据集。

Method: 提出一种基于InfoNCE损失的内在奖励机制，该机制通过课程式(constant curriculum)递增难度的负工具调用样本提供对比训练信号，以无监督方式激励模型学习正确的缩放行为。此外，构建了可扩展的合成Muffin&Chihuahua(M&C)数据集，其中图像由格子网格组成，每个格子展示松饼或吉娃娃，并带有独特的感兴趣区域(ROI)标注，用于精确评估模型缩放到正确位置的准确率，并分析缩放候选区域与最终任务表现的指标相关性。

Result: 在V*、HRBench和MME-RealWorld三个基准上，该方法在无需SFT前提下与基线竞争力相当且训练更高效；作为SFT的直接替代方案时在所有基线上均取得最优性能。基于M&C数据集的指标相关性分析表明，缩放区域的召回率(recall)与最终任务性能的相关性最强。

Conclusion: 证明了MLLM中缩放工具的使用可以通过无监督的对比式内在奖励习得，无需额外的标注或SFT预热阶段，从而显著提升训练效率；同时提出衡量缩放能力的可扩展合成数据集，并揭示召回率作为关键优化目标的方向性指导。

Abstract: Using a zoom-in tool is an important foundational part of modern visual agents, because it allows to efficiently handle tasks involving high-resolution images. Most previous methods need an extensive warm-start supervised fine-tuning phase for teaching models zoom-in. We show that this is not necessary by proposing a new intrinsic reward for learning tool use in MLLMs without the need for additional labels or warm-start SFT. Our InfoNCE-style reward uses a curriculum of increasingly hard negative tool calls as a contrastive training signal. Empirical experiments on $V^*$, HRBench and MME-RealWorld show that our approach is competitive while being more efficient. When used as a drop-in replacement for SFT, we even outperform all baselines. To directly measure the zoom-in ability of models, we further introduce the scalable synthetic Muffin&Chihuahua (M&C) dataset. Each image consists of a grid with every cell either showing a muffin or chihuahua. Leveraging the M&C dataset's unique region of interest labels, we find that recall is the metric that most strongly correlates the zoom-in region with final task performance. Our model and code for reproduction is publicly available under https://github.com/UKPLab/emnlp2026-zoom-in

</details>


### [16] [ProgResViT: Progressive Resolution and Width for Adaptive Vision Transformers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03216)
*Ali Hojjat,Janek Haberer,Olaf Landsiedel*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出ProgResViT，一种输入自适应的视觉Transformer模型，通过多轮渐进式推理（逐步增加输入分辨率和子网络宽度）实现自适应计算。模型采用共享骨干网络，并引入Progress-Conditioned Soft Gating（PSG）机制，根据当前推理轮次、网络块和输入分辨率来调节token融合与层输出。实验表明在图像分类上优于自适应宽度、深度和动态token基线，配合知识蒸馏达到84.9% top-1准确率，并有效适用于自监督DINO表征及下游语义分割任务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准Vision Transformer对所有图像使用固定的输入分辨率和模型宽度进行计算，但许多图像实际上可以用更少的计算量完成分类。作者希望针对不同图像按需分配计算资源，以实现更好的准确率-计算量权衡。

Method: ProgResViT采用多轮渐进式推理：第一轮对低分辨率图像使用窄子网络预测；若置信度不足，则保留当前轮已生成的表征，使用更高分辨率输入和更宽子网络继续优化预测。所有轮次共享同一骨干，并提出Progress-Conditioned Soft Gating (PSG)机制，使token融合与层输出条件依赖于当前轮次、网络块和输入分辨率。

Result: ProgResViT在图像分类上的准确率-计算量权衡优于自适应宽度、自适应深度和动态token基线。使用知识蒸馏后，基于DeiT的ProgResViT达到84.9% top-1准确率，在可比评估设置下略超DeiT-III-S的报道准确率。该设计同样在自监督DINO表征和下游语义分割中展现良好效果。

Conclusion: 渐进式分辨率与宽度扩展的设计能有效实现输入自适应推理，在共享骨干下通过PSG机制保持各轮次间表征的连贯性，为ViT提供更优的准确率-计算权衡，并具有良好的泛化能力（适用于自监督与密集预测任务）。

Abstract: Vision Transformers (ViTs) typically process every image using a fixed input resolution and model width, even though many images can be classified with substantially less computation. We introduce ProgResViT, an input-adaptive ViT that performs inference progressively across multiple rounds. The first round processes a low-resolution image with a narrow subnetwork. Inference terminates when the prediction is sufficiently confident; otherwise, the model reuses the representations produced in the current round and proceeds with a higher-resolution input and a wider subnetwork to refine its prediction. As all rounds share a single backbone, we propose Progress-Conditioned Soft Gating (PSG), which conditions token fusion and layer outputs on the current round, block, and input resolution. On image classification, applying ProgResViT to DeiT yields better accuracy-compute trade-offs than adaptive-width, adaptive-depth, and dynamic-token baselines. With knowledge distillation, a DeiT-based ProgResViT achieves 84.9% top-1 accuracy, slightly exceeding the reported DeiT-III-S accuracy under a comparable evaluation setting. We show that the same design also provides favorable accuracy-compute trade-offs for self-supervised DINO representations and downstream semantic segmentation. Code is available at https://github.com/ds-kiel/ProgResViT.

</details>


### [17] [Counting Animals in Camera-Traps Image Sequences without Count Labels: Winning Solution to the iWildCam 2021 Challenge](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03233)
*Fagner Cunha,Juan G. Colonna,Eulanda M. dos Santos*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文介绍了MaxBoxCount方法，即iWildCam 2021挑战赛的冠军解决方案。该方法结合了强物种分类模型与基于MegaDetector检测的简单计数启发式，用于估计相机陷阱图像序列中不同个体的数量，且无需人工计数的标注数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 相机陷阱已成为野生动物监测的重要工具，但大多数现有工作聚焦于物种识别。许多生态应用需要估算短图像序列中出现的唯一个体数。该任务具有两大挑战：相机陷阱以约每秒一帧的突发模式采集图像，时间不连续导致传统多目标跟踪方法不可靠；手动收集个体计数标注成本极其高昂。因此需要一种无需计数标注即可估算个体数量的方法。

Method: 提出的MaxBoxCount方法由两部分组成：(1) 一个强健的物种分类流水线；(2) 一个基于MegaDetector目标检测结果的简单而有效的计数启发式算法，用于估计序列中存在的唯一个体数量。该方法无需训练过程中提供计数标注，符合真实世界中标注稀缺的约束条件。

Result: 该方法在iWildCam 2021挑战赛中获得了冠军成绩，该挑战赛提供了在现实标注约束下进行序列级动物计数的基准（训练时无计数标注可用）。MaxBoxCount在无需计数标注的前提下成功实现了个体数量的有效估计。

Conclusion: MaxBoxCount证明了结合强大的检测/分类工具与简单启发式策略，即使在没有显式计数监督的情况下，也能有效解决相机陷阱图像序列中的个体计数问题，为野生动物生态监测提供了可行的自动化解决方案。

Abstract: Camera traps have become an essential tool for wildlife monitoring, motivating the development of computer vision methods for the automated extraction of information from these data. While most prior work has focused on species identification, many ecological applications also require estimating the number of unique individuals appearing across short image sequences. This task is particularly challenging because camera traps typically acquire bursts of images at approximately one frame per second, creating large temporal discontinuities that may make conventional multi-object tracking methods unreliable, and because manually collecting individual count annotations is prohibitively expensive. In this work, we describe the winning solution to the iWildCam 2021 Challenge, which introduced a benchmark for counting animals at the sequence level under realistic annotation constraints where count annotations are unavailable for training. Our approach, MaxBoxCount, combines a strong species classification pipeline with a simple yet effective counting heuristic based on MegaDetector detections to estimate the number of unique individuals without requiring count annotations. Code is available at https://github.com/alcunha/iwildcam2021ufam.

</details>


### [18] [An Ensemble-Based Self-Taught Learning Approach for Parking Space Classification Under Limited Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03258)
*Lucas de Oliveira Cunha,Joelton Deonei Gotz,Paulo Lisboa de Almeida,Andre Gustavo Hochuli*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种基于卷积自编码器无监督表征学习的自教学习框架用于停车位分类，通过异构自编码器集成显著减少标注需求并提升跨域鲁棒性，在PKLot和CNRPark基准上实现93%-96%的准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 停车位分类是智能交通系统中的基础任务，但现有深度学习方法依赖大量标注数据且在异构环境下的泛化能力有限。

Method: 采用自教学习框架，基于卷积自编码器从无标签数据学习可迁移的视觉表征，将学习到的编码器作为固定特征提取器用于有监督分类；为了增强鲁棒性和缓解架构偏差，使用异构自编码器集成（多个独立分类头，推理时融合预测结果）。

Result: 在PKLot和CNRPark基准上的跨数据集评估表明，所提出的集成策略显著降低了标注需求，在明显领域偏移下提升了鲁棒性，数据受限场景下准确率达到93%-96%。

Conclusion: 异构自编码器集成与无监督表征学习相结合的有效策略，能够在标注样本稀少的情况下实现高精度的跨域停车位分类，为数据受限的智能交通视觉任务提供了一种可行方案。

Abstract: Parking spot classification is a fundamental task in intelligent transportation systems, yet most deep learning approaches rely on large amounts of annotated data and exhibit limited generalization across heterogeneous environments. To address these limitations, we investigate a self-taught learning framework based on unsupervised representation learning with convolutional autoencoders. The proposed approach learns transferable visual representations from unlabeled data and reuses the learned encoders as fixed feature extractors for supervised classification with limited annotated samples in the target domain. To further enhance robustness and mitigate architectural bias, an ensemble of heterogeneous autoencoders is employed, with independent classifier heads and prediction fusion at inference time. Experiments conducted on the PKLot and CNRPark benchmarks under cross-dataset evaluation protocols show that the proposed ensemble-based strategy substantially reduces annotation requirements while improving robustness under significant domain shifts, achieving accuracies between 93\% and 96\% in data-constrained scenarios.

</details>


### [19] [MedQA-MM: Shortcuts Behind Medical Visual Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03261)
*Benlu Wang,Yifan Zhang,Jiaqing Yu,Chin Siang Ong,Juncheng Huang,Zhuohao Li,Zhenyu Zhang,Arman Cohan,Hong Yu,Zonghai Yao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文研究了医学多模态多项选择题中的"推理膨胀"现象——即模型可能通过题目中的非预期线索（如选项措辞、非视觉临床文本、图像文字、人工标注或设备伪影）而非预期图像发现来获得正确答案。基于六项医学多模态MCQ数据集的多维审计，发现全输入准确率为62.63%，而纯文本和纯选项设置分别可达53.96%和29.71%，移除特定线索可显著降低准确率。作者构建了MedQA-MM子集以缓解捷径，并强调医学图像推理声明需要路径级别证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的基准评分仅以最终答案计分，不区分得出答案的推理路径。在医学多模态多选题中，正确答案可能来自预期的图像发现，也可能来自基准中残留的各种线索（如选项措辞、临床文本、可见图像文字、人工标注或设备伪影）。这种分数层面的过度解读被称为"推理膨胀"，需要系统性地将候选线索与行为证据分离。

Method: 作者跨六个医学多模态多选题数据集展开研究，通过：(1)提示侧和图像侧审计来分离候选线索；(2)模态消融实验（全输入vs纯文本vs纯选项）；(3)匹配修复以保持医学目标和答案解析不变。具体基于13种配置的开放模型面板，系统移除长度差距、绝对/醒目和空间/介词线索来检验其影响，并构建了1,000条目的捷径缓解子集MedQA-MM。

Result: 全输入准确率为62.63%，纯文本设置为53.96%，纯选项设置为29.71%。移除长度差距、绝对/醒目和空间/介词线索分别使准确率下降6.58、3.50和4.77个百分点。在MedQA-MM子集中，纯文本和纯选项准确率分别降至5.21%和12.33%，表明捷径被有效缓解。

Conclusion: 模型并非完全不用图像，但医学图像推理的声明必须基于路径级别的证据（即证明答案确实通过预期的图像输入路径得出），而非仅凭最终分数。此研究为医学多模态评估提供了更严谨的方法论框架。

Abstract: A benchmark score credits final answers, but not the route by which an item can be answered. In medical multimodal multiple-choice questions (MCQs), this distinction matters because a correct answer can be supported by the intended image finding or by benchmark-preserved cues in the wording of answers, non-visual clinical text, visible image text, artificial annotations, or device/context artifacts. We call the resulting score-level overinterpretation reasoning inflation. Here, a route is an observable input path that can support answer selection, not a claim about the model's hidden cognition. Across six medical multimodal MCQ datasets, we separate candidate cues from behavioral evidence through prompt- and image-side audits, modality ablations, and matched repairs that preserve the medical target and answer key. In a 13-configuration open-model panel, full-input accuracy is 62.63%, while text-only and options-only settings achieve 53.96% and 29.71%, respectively. Removing length-gap, absolute/conspicuous, and spatial/prepositional cues lowers accuracy by 6.58, 3.50, and 4.77 percentage points. We also construct MedQA-MM, a 1,000-item shortcut-mitigated subset, where text-only and options-only accuracy fall to 5.21% and 12.33%. This does not imply that models never use images; it shows that medical image-reasoning claims require route-level evidence.

</details>


### [20] [Tensor-based Brain Surface Modeling and Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03302)
*Moo K. Chung,Keith J. Worsley,Steve Robbins,Alan C. Evans*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种统一的张量形态测量法计算框架，用于基于MRI检测两组临床人群之间的大脑皮层表面形状差异。该方法将表面建模、表面数据平滑和统计分析整合在一个数学框架中，利用基于Laplace-Beltrami算子显式估计的扩散平滑来提升信噪比，并实现对皮层局部表面积和曲率差异的定位。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大脑皮层是具有高度卷曲的二维薄片结构，不同临床群体间的局部表面积和曲率可能存在差异，且这种差异在皮层上并非均匀分布。然而现有方法缺乏一个将表面建模、平滑与统计整合的统一框架，难以精确、可靠地定位快速结构差异的区域。因此需要发展一种统一的计算方法以提高检测精度并增强信噪比。

Method: 提出一种统一的基于张量形态测量（tensor-based morphometry）框架，核心包括：(1) 表面建模用于构建皮层表面的几何表征；(2) 基于Laplace-Beltrami算子显式估计的扩散平滑技术，将平滑直接作用于皮层表面度量以提升信噪比；(3) 在统一的数学框架内对表面度量（表面积与曲率）进行统计分析，从而定位两组间结构差异最显著的区域。

Result: 作为实例，该方法成功应用于对一组儿童纵向采集的脑影像中灰质组织生长与丢失区域的皮层定位，验证了该张量表面形态测量法在检测脑表面形状变化方面的可行性和有效性。

Conclusion: 该研究提供了一种将表面建模、平滑和统计集成于同一数学框架的张量形态测量方法，能够精确地定位临床组间皮层表面局部形状差异，并有望广泛应用于脑发育和神经疾病相关的脑结构变化研究。

Abstract: We present a unified computational approach to tensor-based morphometry in detecting the brain surface shape differences between two clinical groups based on magnetic resonance images. Our approach is novel in a sense that we combined surface modeling, surface data smoothing and statistical analysis in a coherent unified mathematical framework. The cerebral cortex has the topology of a 2D highly convoluted sheet. Between two different clinical groups, the local surface area and curvature of the cortex may differ. It is highly likely that such surface shape differences are not uniform over the whole cortex. By computing how such surface metrics differ, the regions of the most rapid structural differences can be localized. To increase the signal to noise ratio, diffusion smoothing based on the explicit estimation of Laplace-Beltrami operator has been developed and applied to the surface metrics. As an illustration, we demonstrate how this new tensor-based surface morphometry can be applied in localizing the cortical regions of the gray matter tissue growth and loss in the brain images longitudinally collected in the group of children.

</details>


### [21] [Laplacian Frequency Hierarchies for Efficient 3D Gaussian Splatting Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03334)
*Yixiong Yang,Sisheng Zhang,Qingsong Yan,Shaohuai Shi,Qiang Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出拉普拉斯频率层次（Laplacian Frequency Hierarchies）方案，通过将拉普拉斯图像分解与从粗到细的分频段训练相结合，在3D高斯溅射训练中降低活跃图元数量，从而加速训练收敛。该方法以即插即用的方式与现有强基线（如Taming-3DGS和FastGS）正交兼容，在1K和4K设置下分别取得最高1.73x/1.21x和1.74x/1.33x的训练加速，且分辨率越高、场景越复杂，收益越明显。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 3D高斯溅射训练的瓶颈在于高斯图元持续增长，导致优化开销增加、收敛缓慢，尤其在高分辨率场景下问题更为突出。现有方法未能有效缓解图元累积带来的训练负担，因此需要一种简单高效的训练方案来降低活跃图元数量、加速收敛。

Method: 提出Laplacian Frequency Hierarchies方法，核心思路包括：(1) 将拉普拉斯图像分解与从粗到细的分频段训练相结合；(2) 先拟合低频结构，然后将对应的低频频段高斯场归档，使后续高斯场专注于优化高频残差，无需承载全部图元负担；(3) 推理阶段在图像域通过拉普拉斯式重建将各频段渲染分量组合起来。该方法减少了训练过程中的活跃高斯数量，从而降低优化开销并加速训练，且具备即插即用特性，可与既有3DGS加速方法正交组合。

Result: 该方案在1K设置下对Taming-3DGS和FastGS分别实现平均1.73x和1.21x的加速，在4K设置下分别实现1.74x和1.33x的加速；在更具挑战性的场景中获得更大的加速收益，且高分辨率下优势愈发显著，同时在重建质量上保持竞争力。

Conclusion: 拉普拉斯频率层次方法通过频段分离与高斯场归档策略，有效解决了3DGS训练中图元增长导致的效率和收敛问题。作为正交于现有加速技术的即插即用方案，它能够在保持竞争力的重建质量的同时显著提升训练速度，尤其在分辨率越高、场景越复杂的条件下收益越突出，为3DGS训练加速提供了新的有效途径。

Abstract: A key bottleneck in 3D Gaussian Splatting training is the continual growth of Gaussian primitives, which increases optimization cost and slows convergence, especially at high resolutions. We propose Laplacian Frequency Hierarchies, a simple yet efficient 3DGS scheme that combines Laplacian image decomposition with coarse-to-fine, frequency-staged training. After fitting lower-frequency structure, we archive the corresponding Gaussian field so that subsequent fields can optimize higher-frequency residuals without carrying the full primitive burden, and we compose the rendered components in the image domain via a Laplacian-style reconstruction at inference time. This design reduces the number of active Gaussians during training, thereby lowering optimization overhead and accelerating training. The proposed scheme is plug-and-play and orthogonal to prior 3DGS accelerations: it can be directly combined with strong backbones such as Taming-3DGS and FastGS to improve training speed with competitive reconstruction quality. It achieves average speedups of 1.73x and 1.21x at 1K setting, and 1.74x and 1.33x at 4K setting on Taming-3DGS and FastGS, with larger gains on more challenging scenes and increasingly pronounced benefits at higher resolutions.

</details>


### [22] [When Depth Hurts: Reliability-Aware Geometry Distillation for Depth-Free RGB-D Salient Object Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03378)
*Xuehao Wang,Jiaxin Hua,Runmei Li,Zhenyu Wu,Chenglizhao Chen,Ke Gu,Aimin Hao*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 该论文提出了一种无需传感器深度（RGB-D）的可靠性感知几何蒸馏框架，用于RGB-D显著目标检测。训练阶段利用冻结的Depth Anything V2模型作为教师，将密集相对几何、层次空间注意和边界结构蒸馏到紧凑的边缘感知几何分支中；采用池化双向交互对齐几何与外观，并通过像素级可靠性估计器选择性注入与RGB表征兼容的几何信息。推理阶段移除教师，仅使用RGB网络。在36项指标-数据集对比中取得26项最佳或并列最佳结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RGB-D显著目标检测中传感器深度并非始终可靠，缺失区域、模糊边界和结构伪影会通过多模态融合传播，使RGB-D检测器在某些情况下甚至不如仅用RGB的方法。现有质量感知方法虽能调节观测深度，但仍依赖同一可能缺陷的模态。作者希望利用可靠的深度先验知识（而非数据集提供的传感器深度）来提升RGB-D SOD性能，且训练和推理均不依赖数据集深度。

Method: 提出一个两阶段的可靠性感知几何蒸馏框架：1) 训练阶段使用冻结的Depth Anything V2当作教师模型，将密集相对几何、层次空间注意力及边界结构蒸馏给一个紧凑的边缘感知几何分支；2) 通过池化双向交互（pooled bidirectional interaction）将对齐几何与外观特征；3) 设计像素级可靠性估计器，选择性注入与当前RGB表示兼容的几何信息。训练完成后移除教师，得到一个纯RGB推理网络。

Result: 在2,985对RGB-掩码对上训练，该方法在36项指标-数据集对比中（与十种近期RGB-D SOD方法对比）取得26项最佳或并列最佳，其中在ReDWeb-S上相对MAE降低13.4%。在DUTS-TR上重训练时，在PASCAL-S上将最强先前的F-measure提升4.2%，表明蒸馏的几何知识可跨传感器与数据集域迁移。

Conclusion: 通过将可靠的预训练深度模型作为训练时教师来蒸馏几何知识而非依赖有缺陷的传感器深度，可以在RGB-D SOD基准上取得超越多数现有RGB-D方法的效果，同时推理阶段仅需RGB输入。该方法具备域泛化能力，证明了深度蒸馏知识在跨数据集、跨传感器场景下的有效性。

Abstract: Depth can resolve appearance ambiguity in RGB-D salient object detection (SOD), yet sensor depth is not uniformly reliable. Missing regions, blurred boundaries, and structural artifacts can propagate through multimodal fusion and make an RGB-D detector less accurate than its RGB-only counterpart. Existing quality-aware approaches regulate observed depth but remain dependent on the same potentially defective modality. We propose \method, a reliability-aware geometry distillation framework developed for RGB-D SOD benchmarks without using dataset-provided depth during training or inference. A frozen Depth Anything V2 model serves only as a training-time teacher, transferring dense relative geometry, hierarchical spatial attention, and boundary structure to a compact edge-aware geometry branch. Pooled bidirectional interaction aligns geometry with appearance, and a pixel-wise reliability estimator selectively injects geometry that is compatible with the current RGB representation. The teacher is removed after training, leaving an RGB-only inference network. Trained on 2,985 RGB-mask pairs, \method{} achieves the best or tied-best result in 26 of 36 metric-dataset comparisons against ten recent RGB-D SOD methods, including a 13.4\% relative MAE reduction on ReDWeb-S. When retrained on DUTS-TR, it also improves the strongest prior $F$-measure by 4.2\% on PASCAL-S, showing that the distilled geometry transfers beyond a particular sensor or dataset domain. Code will be released upon publication.

</details>


### [23] [FoRIS: Progressive Foreground Refinement for Training-Free In-Context Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03384)
*Ming Hu,Jianfu Yin,Mingyu Dou,Miaomiao Zhang,Yao Wang,Cong Hu,Bingliang Hu,Quan Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出FoRIS，一种无需训练的上下文分割（In-Context Segmentation）框架。与以往直接通过参考-查询匹配预测最终掩码的方式不同，FoRIS将ICS重新视角化为一个从粗到细的渐进式细化过程，包含三个关键阶段：前景纯化（抑制背景干扰）、前景定位（定位判别性目标区域）、前景整合（通过语义聚合恢复完整前景结构）。实验表明，FoRIS在语义分割和部分分割任务上均达到SOTA水平，在1-shot和5-shot设置下分别比现有方法平均提升4.5和4.8个mIoU点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的In-Context Segmentation方法通常直接通过参考图像与查询图像之间的匹配来预测最终分割掩码，这种方法忽略了分割任务的渐进特性。作者希望从更经典的粗到细分割视角重新审视ICS问题，通过渐进式细化来克服直接匹配带来的前景响应模糊、结构不完整等问题。

Method: FoRIS是一个无需训练的上下文分割框架，采用三段式渐进细化策略：（1）前景纯化（Foreground Purification）：抑制背景干扰，获取干净的前景响应；（2）前景定位（Foreground Localization）：在净化后的响应中定位具有判别性的目标区域；（3）前景整合（Foreground Consolidation）：通过语义聚合恢复完整的前景结构，最终生成精确完整的分割掩码。

Result: FoRIS在语义分割和部分分割任务上均取得了SOTA性能，在1-shot设置下平均比现有方法提升4.5个mIoU点，在5-shot设置下平均提升4.8个mIoU点。

Conclusion: 通过将ICS重新定义为由粗到细的渐进细化过程，FoRIS证明了无需额外训练即可显著提升上下文分割性能。这种三步式的前景处理框架有效解决了直接匹配策略中前景响应不确定和结构不完整的问题，为ICS领域提供了一个简单而有效的新范式。

Abstract: In-Context Segmentation (ICS) aims to precisely segment arbitrary semantic concepts, such as objects or parts, given one or a few annotated visual exemplars. In this paper, we revisit ICS from a more classical segmentation perspective, viewing it as a coarse-to-fine progressive refinement process. Rather than directly predicting the final mask through reference-query matching, we progressively refine the segmentation from coarse and ambiguous foreground responses to precise and complete foreground structures. Building upon this perspective, we propose a training-free in-context segmentation framework, termed FoRIS. Specifically, FoRIS consists of three key stages: Foreground Purification, Foreground Localization, and Foreground Consolidation, which progressively suppress background distractions, localize discriminative target regions, and recover complete foreground structures through semantic aggregation. Experimental results demonstrate that FoRIS achieves SOTA performance across semantic and part segmentation tasks, with average improvements of 4.5 and 4.8 mIoU points over existing approaches in the 1-shot and 5-shot settings, respectively. Code: https://github.com/Xi-Mu-Yu/FoRIS.

</details>


### [24] [Exploring the Potential of Contrastive Language-Image Pre-training for Multi-Source Remote Sensing Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03391)
*Xiangyang Miao,Kelu Yao,Yekai Huang,Xiaogang Xu,Junxiao Xue,Minjun Shen,Chenghui Lv,Shanji Liu,Yaying Chen,Chao Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniRSCLIP，一个支持多源传感器（RGB、SAR、MSI、HSI）输入的端到端遥感视觉-语言对比学习框架。其核心创新是光谱-空间基分解（SSBD），将任意通道适配形式化为基重组问题，在保持预训练CLIP视觉知识的同时扩展异构传感器支持；并引入光谱上下文感知的掩码对比学习抑制模态冗余特征，构建了首个大规模多模态遥感图像-文本语料库OmniRS5M。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有遥感对比学习方法大多基于面向RGB的CLIP架构，难以利用异构传感器（如SAR、多光谱MSI、高光谱HSI）的数据，限制了多模态遥感视觉-语言理解能力。

Method: 1）提出Spectral-Spatial Basis Decomposition (SSBD)：将任意通道适配形式化为基重组问题，预训练CLIP的patch嵌入提供可迁移的空间基，波长条件系数在受限的视觉先验空间内生成传感器特定的嵌入核；2）提出光谱上下文感知的掩码对比学习方案，抑制模态特异性冗余特征、增强细粒度图文对齐；3）构建OmniRS5M，首个覆盖RGB、SAR、MSI和HSI的大规模遥感图像-文本语料库。

Result: 在检索、零样本分类和语义定位任务上的实验表明，OmniRSCLIP在保持强RGB域性能的同时，有效将CLIP推广到异构遥感模态。

Conclusion: 通过基分解方式在不破坏预训练视觉知识的前提下实现异构传感器适配，并以统一的图文语义空间对齐多模态数据，验证了该方法在多模态遥感理解中的有效性。

Abstract: Contrastive language-image learning (CLIP) has become a key paradigm for remote sensing vision-language understanding. However, existing remote sensing contrastive learning methods are mostly built on RGB-oriented CLIP architectures, making it difficult to exploit heterogeneous sensors such as SAR, multi-spectral imaging (MSI), and hyperspectral imaging (HSI). To address this limitation, we propose OmniRSCLIP, an end-to-end contrastive learning framework that supports multi-source sensor inputs for remote sensing vision-language modeling. The key idea is to extend CLIP beyond its fixed RGB input interface without breaking the pretrained visual knowledge. To this end, OmniRSCLIP introduces Spectral-Spatial Basis Decomposition (SSBD), which formulates arbitrary-channel adaptation as a basis recomposition problem: pretrained CLIP patch embeddings provide transferable spatial bases, while wavelength-conditioned coefficients span sensor-specific embedding kernels within a constrained visual prior space. This design avoids forcing heterogeneous sensors into a fixed-channel input space, while aligning them in a unified image-text semantic space. We further introduce a spectral-context-aware mask-based contrastive learning scheme to suppress modality-specific redundant features and enhance fine-grained image-text alignment. Finally, to support multi-modal training, we construct OmniRS5M, the first large-scale remote sensing image-text corpus covering RGB, SAR, MSI, and HSI. Experiments on retrieval, zero-shot classification, and semantic localization show that OmniRSCLIP preserves strong RGB-domain performance while effectively extending CLIP to heterogeneous remote sensing modalities.

</details>


### [25] [Neural-Collapse-guided Task-Free Continual Anomaly Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03406)
*Xiaotong Kong,Chaoyang Song,Ziai Zhou,Jinxia Zhang,Kanjian Zhang,Haikun Wei*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出NC-TFAD，一个基于神经坍缩（Neural Collapse）启发的几何驱动框架，用于解决无任务边界（task-free）的持续工业异常检测问题。该框架冻结预训练骨干网络，将流式特征对齐到单纯形等角紧框架（ETF）原型空间，通过合成异常样本、类间/类内正则化及焦点神经坍缩对比（FNCC）损失抑制表征漂移，并利用正常补丁原型引导的定位分支生成无像素标注的异常热图，在MVTec AD和VisA上超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 真实工业制造环境中的数据分布存在不可预测的漂移，而现有持续异常检测方法依赖任务边界假设，在实际场景中不适用。因此需要一种无需任务边界、能够在非平稳数据流中持续学习且保持稳定表征的工业异常检测方法。

Method: NC-TFAD包含以下核心组件：(1) 冻结预训练骨干网络，将流式特征对齐到单纯形ETF原型空间以稳定表征几何；(2) 生成合成异常样本作为辅助锚点，满足无真实异常条件下的神经坍缩几何构建；(3) 引入类间与类内正则化及FNCC损失，抑制表征漂移并增强正常-异常可分性；(4) 正常补丁原型引导的定位分支，构建校准的逐块偏差图并结合弱自注意力先验生成异常热图，无需像素级标注。

Result: 在MVTec AD和VisA两个基准数据集上，NC-TFAD在任务自由持续学习协议下的图像级检测和像素级定位任务中，均一致性地优于从通用视觉领域适配的代表性任务自由持续学习方法以及统一的异常检测基线方法。

Conclusion: 几何驱动建模为真实工业应用中的任务自由持续异常检测提供了一种有效且鲁棒的解决方案，验证了利用神经坍缩原理和几何约束来稳定非平稳流数据表征的可行性。

Abstract: Recent years have witnessed growing interest in continual anomaly detection for industrial visual inspection. However, real-world manufacturing environments exhibit unpredictable shifts in data distributions, rendering task-dependent continual learning assumptions impractical. To address this limitation, we formulate industrial anomaly detection as a task-free continual learning problem and propose NC-TFAD, a neural-collapse-inspired, geometry-driven framework for learning from non-stationary data streams without task boundaries. NC-TFAD freezes a pretrained backbone and aligns streaming features to a simplex Equiangular Tight Frame (ETF) prototype space to stabilize representation geometry under non-stationary streams. To satisfy the NC-inspired geometric construction in the absence of real anomalies, we generate synthetic anomaly samples as auxiliary anchors during training. Building on this geometry, we further introduce inter- and intra-class regularization together with a Focal Neural Collapse Contrastive (FNCC) loss to suppress representation drift and improve normal-anomaly separability. Finally, a normal-patch-prototype-guided localization branch constructs calibrated patch-wise deviation maps from normal training samples and fuses them with a weak self-attention prior, producing anomaly heatmaps without pixel-level annotations. Extensive experiments on MVTec AD and VisA show that NC-TFAD consistently outperforms representative task-free continual learning methods adapted from general vision, as well as unified anomaly detection baselines, in both image-level detection and pixel-level localization under the task-free continual learning protocol. These results highlight that geometry-driven modeling offers an effective and robust solution for task-free continual anomaly detection in real-world industrial applications.

</details>


### [26] [Mudragen: Geometrically Supervised Generation of Interacting Two-Hand Mudras for Preserving Indian Classical Dance Heritage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03415)
*Jagadish Kashinath Kamble,Jayanta Mukhopadhyay,Debaditya Roy,Partha Pratim Das*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: MudraGen是一个基于条件扩散框架的模型，用于从印度古典舞Bharatanatyam中合成Samyukta Hasta Mudras（双手互动的舞蹈手势）的逼真RGB图像，通过三个几何感知目标（关键点损失、关节偏移损失、形状一致性）来确保双手的精确协调、解剖有效性和文化细微差别，在视觉真实性、解剖正确性和手势结构保持方面超越现有方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 印度古典舞蹈手势的自动生成对舞蹈传承与保护至关重要。但此类数据集天然低资源（low-resource），且许多mudras的梵文规范定义缺乏精确文本描述，限制了传统文本条件图像生成模型的效果。手势包含双手交互，远比简单手部符号或单手手势复杂，现有方法无法捕捉交互手的精确协调、解剖有效性和文化细节。

Method: 提出MudraGen，一个几何感知监督的条件扩散框架，针对Bharatanatyam中Samyukta Hasta Mudras的双手交互手势合成。引入三个几何感知目标：(1) Keypoint Loss用于3D关节对齐；(2) Joint Offset Loss用于确保双手间的空间连贯性；(3) Shape Consistency作为解剖正则化器，在允许独立手姿势的同时保持一致的形态。三目标共同引导扩散模型生成解剖合理且协调良好的手部配置。

Result: 实验表明MudraGen在视觉真实感、解剖正确性和精细手姿势结构保持方面均超越现有最先进生成方法，能够忠实再现复杂的Samyukta Hasta mudras，并产生文化根基扎实且结构一致的手势。

Conclusion: MudraGen通过几何感知监督成功解决了低资源条件下双手交互舞蹈手势生成的难题，其生成结果具有文化真实性和结构一致性，展示了在文化遗产保存与舞蹈教育中的实际应用价值。

Abstract: Automatic generation of hand gestures is essential for the transmission of Indian classical dance and critical for its preservation. Indian classical dance gesture datasets are inherently low-resource, and the canonical Sanskrit definitions of many mudras lack precise textual descriptions, limiting the effectiveness of conventional text-conditioned image generation models. We present \textbf{MudraGen}, a conditional diffusion framework that synthesizes realistic RGB images of \textit{Samyukta Hasta Mudras} -- interactive two-hand gestures from Bharatanatyam (an Indian classical dance form). Unlike prior work on simple hand signs or single-hand gestures, MudraGen introduces geometry-aware supervision to capture the precise coordination, anatomical validity, and cultural nuance of interacting hands. We formulate three geometry-aware objectives: Keypoint Loss for 3D joint alignment, Joint Offset Loss for inter-hand spatial coherence, and Shape Consistency, which serves as an anatomical regularizer by encouraging consistent hand morphology while allowing independent hand poses. Together, these objectives guide the diffusion model toward anatomically plausible and well-coordinated hand configurations, enabling the synthesis of photorealistic and pose-accurate gesture images. Experimental results show that MudraGen surpasses existing state-of-the-art generative approaches in visual realism, anatomical correctness, and preservation of fine hand-pose structure, enabling faithful reproduction of complex Samyukta Hasta mudras. Beyond quantitative gains, its ability to generate culturally grounded and structurally consistent gestures highlights practical applications in cultural preservation and dance education.

</details>


### [27] [When Do Frozen VLMs Respond to Image-Free Object-Token Edits? An Answer-Key-Free Protocol and What It Reveals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03429)
*Wonbin Son,Gyumun Choi,Junil Seo,Seungmin Rho,Mi Young Lee,Hyungjoon Kim*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出一种无需答案标注的协议，用来探究冻结视觉语言模型（VLM）对"对象级token编辑"（即在模型输入前将场景抽象为对象token而非输入原始图像）何时产生响应。发现在密集场景中显式编辑教学而非普通VQA训练才能激发响应，响应受token清洁度和密度调控，且图像无关的token路径保留了基线92-96%的自由文本VQA性能。三个结构在遥感数据集和三个冻结LM骨干上符号一致保持，相关代码和数据已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 回答关于场景的what-if查询通常需要通过文本注入假设或用生成模型重绘场景。作者提出另一种思路：在表征层面（模型输入之前）进行编辑——将图像抽象为一组对象级token，原始图像完全不进入VLM。但该设计依赖一个开放问题：冻结的VLM何时会对这类token编辑真正产生响应？此外，现有评估缺乏无需答案标注的协议。

Method: 提出无需答案关键字（answer-key-free）的协议：不标注编辑后的答案，只对逻辑上能确定答案的编辑进行评分，并通过逆转每个可评分的选择来进行自审计。在此协议基础上设计了对象级token编辑的探测实验，涉及三种编辑操作，对比显式编辑教学与普通VQA训练，评估了可部署检测器+分割器token与oracle设置的表现，并使用三个冻结LM骨干在iSAID和VRSBench两个遥感数据集上验证。

Result: 协议揭示了三个结构：（1）响应不是自发的——显式编辑教学（而非普通VQA训练）能在密集场景中激发响应，并在稀疏场景中放大响应，在所有三种操作上均如此；（2）一旦响应开启，受token清洁度和密度制约，可部署的检测器+分割器token与oracle相当，且在VRSBench上优于oracle；（3）读取是可分离的轴线——图像无关的token路线保留了匹配的patch-token基线92-96%的自由文本VQA能力，且答案可测量地依赖于token。三个结构在两个遥感数据集和三个冻结LM骨干上符号一致保持。

Conclusion: 冻结VLM对对象级token编辑的响应具有可预测的结构规律：需要显式编辑教学才能激发，受token清洁度与密度调控，且图像无关路线保留了绝大部分的VQA能力。该理解展示了表征层编辑作为无生成模型what-if查询的可行路径，并提供了可复现的探针生成器、记录、评判日志和代码。

Abstract: Answering what-if queries about a scene with a VLM usually means injecting the assumption as text or repainting the scene with a generative model. We instead move the edit to the representation level, before the model input. The image is abstracted into a set of object-level tokens, and the original image never enters the VLM. This design rests on an open question: when do frozen VLMs actually respond to such token edits? We introduce an answer-key-free protocol: no post-edit answer is annotated. It scores edits whose answers are logically determined, and audits itself by reversing each scoreable choice. The protocol reveals three structures. The response is not free: explicit edit teaching, not ordinary VQA training, produces it in dense scenes and multiplies it in sparse ones, on all three operations. Once on, it is governed by token cleanliness and density, with deployable detector+segmenter tokens competitive with the oracle and outperforming it on VRSBench. And reading is a separable axis: the image-free token route preserves 92-96% of a matched patch-token baseline's free-text VQA, and the answers measurably depend on the tokens. The response, cleanliness, and reading structures are sign-preserved across two remote-sensing datasets (iSAID, VRSBench) and three frozen LM backbones. We release the probe generator, records, judge logs, and code.

</details>


### [28] [OCR-EDR: Rendering-Aware Diagnosis and Repair for Closed-Loop OCR Improvement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03445)
*Linnan Zhao,Kang Liu,Hao Yu,Jiabo Zhan,Chong Sun,Chen Li*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出OCR-EDR（OCR错误诊断与修复）框架，结合渲染一致性分析，从细粒度错误诊断推进到迭代修复，并构建OCRErrBench基准与DocEDR模型，在公式识别上取得显著性能提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有OCR系统在复杂公式、结构化文本和长尾格式上仍易出错，可能出现遗漏或幻觉输出，且需兼容渲染等价编码。现有OCR评估方法主要报告聚合指标，缺乏对个案错误的细粒度分析和性能改进支持。

Method: OCR-EDR框架包含三个核心组件：1) 渲染感知的诊断机制，联合评估OCR预测与其渲染结果是否与源图像一致，保留有效预测（包括渲染等价的正例），同时诊断和定位真实错误；2) 可执行的编辑操作与更新渲染的迭代再评估机制，构成诊断-修复循环；3) 构建OCRErrBench基准数据集（覆盖文本和公式、精确和渲染等价正例及真实错误），并开发DocEDR模型执行该循环。

Result: 在OCRErrBench上DocEDR诊断准确率达94.78%；修复86.23%的错误输入至视觉一致；在DOCRcaseBench上公式Case-F1较DOCR-Inspector-7B提升30.99个百分点；在UniMER-Test上对四个OCR系统的Bad子集公式CDM最高提升4.62个百分点。

Conclusion: OCR-EDR将细粒度OCR分析转化为经过验证的修正和实际性能收益，证明了渲染感知的诊断-修复循环是提升OCR系统，特别是公式识别能力的有效途径。

Abstract: Although document OCR systems perform increasingly well on routine documents, complex formulas, structured text, and long-tail formats remain error-prone. OCR predictions may omit fine-grained content or hallucinate unsupported outputs, while equivalent encodings of the same visible content must be accommodated. Existing OCR evaluation methods mostly report aggregate metrics, offering limited support for analyzing case-level errors and improving OCR performance. We propose OCR-EDR (OCR Error Diagnosis and Repair), a rendering-aware framework that advances from fine-grained diagnosis to iterative repair. Given a source image, an editable OCR prediction, and its rendered image, OCR-EDR first jointly assesses whether the prediction and its rendering are consistent with the source, preserving valid predictions, including rendering-equivalent ones, while diagnosing and localizing genuine errors. It then applies executable edits and may request an updated rendering for iterative reassessment. We construct OCRErrBench from diverse real OCR predictions, covering text and formulas, exact and rendering-equivalent positives, and genuine errors, and develop the DocEDR model to execute the diagnosis--repair loop. On OCRErrBench, DocEDR achieves 94.78% diagnostic accuracy. It repairs 86.23% of erroneous inputs to visual consistency, raises formula Case-F1 by 30.99 percentage points over DOCR-Inspector-7B on DOCRcaseBench, and improves formula CDM by up to 4.62 percentage points on the identified Bad subsets of four OCR systems on UniMER-Test. These results show that OCR-EDR turns fine-grained OCR analysis into verified corrections and performance gains.

</details>


### [29] [Preserving Knowledge across Space and Time for Continual Video Deepfake Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03446)
*Taehoon Kim,Jongwook Choi,Heejae Jo,Byungmin Park,Jongwon Choi*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 提出了一种持续深度伪造视频检测框架MSFD，通过频域分解视频特征为空间、时间和时空模态，并采用跨模态去相关损失保持各模态独立性，以解决现有方法无法适应不断出现的视频伪造模式且遗忘旧任务的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高质量视频深度伪造的持续涌现要求检测器能持续适应新的伪造模式，但现有方法主要针对深度伪造图像设计，无法捕捉视频特有的线索。与仅含空间伪影的图像不同，深度伪造视频在空间和时间轴上均留有独特证据，需要在模型顺序更新过程中对每种模态进行单独保留，而现有方法未考虑这一问题。

Method: MSFD框架在频域中将视频特征显式分解为空间、时间和时空三种模态，实现对每种模态的独立保留以应对不同伪造视频类型对空间和时间线索依赖程度的差异。同时引入跨模态去相关损失，促使时空表示与单模态线索保持正交。

Result: 大量实验表明，MSFD在各种持续深度伪造视频场景中比最先进方法实现了更强的适应能力，并更有效地保持了先前任务的性能。

Conclusion: 通过模态特定的频率蒸馏与跨模态去相关策略，MSFD有效解决了持续深度伪造视频检测中空间/时间线索遗忘的难题，在适应新伪造模式的同时保持了旧任务的检测性能。

Abstract: The continuous emergence of high-quality video deepfakes requires detectors that continually adapt to new forgery patterns, yet existing approaches, which are designed for deepfake images, fail to capture video-specific cues. Unlike deepfake images that contain only spatial artifacts, deepfake videos leave distinct evidence along both spatial and temporal axes, necessitating the separate preservation of each modality during sequential model updates. To overcome this limitation, we introduce a continual deepfake video detection framework, Modality-Specific Frequency Distillation (MSFD), that explicitly decomposes video features into spatial, temporal, and spatiotemporal modalities in the frequency domain. This decomposition enables independent preservation of each modality, as different deepfake video types exhibit varying reliance on spatial and temporal cues across tasks. Furthermore, MSFD adopts a cross-modality decorrelation loss that encourages spatiotemporal representations to remain orthogonal to single-modality cues. Extensive experiments show that our framework achieves stronger adaptation and preserves performance more effectively than state-of-the-art methods across diverse continual deepfake video scenarios.

</details>


### [30] [STARS-GS: Structure-Aware Regularized Gaussian Splatting for Large-Scale Aerial Surface Reconstruction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03447)
*Bocheng Li,Wenjuan Zhang,Jie Pan. Dongxu Han,Xuesong Ma,Yiling Yao,Yaning Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出STARS-GS，一种面向大规模航拍影像三维表面重建的结构感知3D高斯泼溅（3DGS）框架，通过结构感知场景划分、邻域感知高斯组织与自适应表面正则化三大创新，解决了大场景重建中的跨区域拼接伪影、几何约束不足和均匀正则化不适应异构几何结构的问题，在公开基准上将平均F1分数从0.640提升至0.698。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大规模三维表面重建对地理空间测绘和城市建模至关重要，3DGS虽展现出潜力，但现有方法面临三大挑战：场景划分会割裂连续场景元素、几何约束忽略高斯局部组织、均匀正则化难以适应异构几何结构。

Method: STARS-GS包含三个核心组件：(1)结构感知场景划分策略，在分区时保留连续场景结构，并通过边界细化减少跨区域几何不一致和拼接伪影；(2)邻域感知高斯组织，将几何约束从单个原语扩展到其邻域组织，促使高斯更好地贴合局部表面几何；(3)自适应表面正则化，根据局部几何特征动态调整正则化强度，在结构化区域促进几何一致性，同时在非结构化区域保留合理的几何变化。

Result: 在大规模航空摄影测量基准上的大量实验表明，STARS-GS在表面重建任务上持续优于所评估的高斯类方法，平均F1分数从次优方法的0.640提升至0.698，相对提升约9.1%，在几何精度和表面完整性方面均取得有效改进。

Conclusion: STARS-GS通过结构感知的场景划分、邻域感知的高斯组织约束和自适应的表面正则化，系统性地解决了大规模3DGS表面重建中的结构断裂、局部几何约束不足以及正则化不适应性问题，显著提升了重建的几何精度与表面完整性。

Abstract: Large-scale 3D surface reconstruction from aerial imagery is fundamental to geospatial mapping and urban modeling. Recent advances in 3D Gaussian Splatting (3DGS) have demonstrated considerable potential for this task. However, existing methods still face three major challenges in large and complex scenes: scene partitioning may split continuous scene elements across independently optimized sub-regions; geometric constraints mainly focus on the attributes of individual Gaussians while overlooking their local organization; and uniform regularization struggles to accommodate heterogeneous geometric structures. To address these issues, we propose STARS-GS, a structure-aware 3DGS framework for large-scale surface reconstruction. First, we introduce a structure-aware scene partitioning strategy that better preserves continuous scene structures during partitioning and reduces cross-region geometric inconsistencies and stitching artifacts through boundary refinement. Second, we develop neighborhood-aware Gaussian organization that extends geometric constraints from individual primitives to their neighborhood organization, encouraging Gaussians to better conform to local surface geometry. Third, we introduce adaptive surface regularization that adjusts the regularization strength according to local geometric characteristics, promoting geometric consistency in structured regions while preserving plausible variations in unstructured regions. Extensive experiments on large-scale aerial photogrammetry benchmarks demonstrate that STARS-GS consistently outperforms the evaluated Gaussian-based methods in surface reconstruction. It increases the average F1-score from 0.640 for the second-best method to 0.698, corresponding to a relative improvement of approximately 9.1\%, demonstrating effective improvements in geometric accuracy and surface completeness.

</details>


### [31] [Preprocessing Failure and Adversarial Detection in Depthwise-Separable Edge Vision Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03453)
*Jannatul Masruk Mukta,Rifa Sanjida,Adrita Rahman Tory,Md. Saifur Rahman,Khondokar Fida Hasan*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文评估了六种预处理防御方法在residual架构与depthwise-separable CNN（边缘设备主流架构）上的对抗鲁棒性差异，发现depthwise-separable架构在预处理防御下恢复能力显著较差，且这种失效可被转化为一种无需重训练或架构修改的检测机制。同时指出标准图像质量指标不能可靠代理防御有效性，并提供了面向边缘视觉部署的决策框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有预处理防御的基线评估大多基于residual或Inception类架构，而非边缘部署中占主导地位的depthwise-separable CNN。这一未经验证的假设造成了安全评估文献中的空白，需要在实际部署架构上重新审视预处理防御的有效性。

Method: 在两种架构族（residual vs depthwise-separable）上系统评估六种预处理防御方法对抗对抗扰动的表现；通过消融实验区分架构性因素与参数性因素的解释；将预处理对干净样本与对抗样本的扰动差异转化为可测量的检测信号；并检验图像质量指标与防御有效性之间的相关性。

Result: 所有扰动水平和防御组合下，两种depthwise-separable架构始终表现出较差的对抗恢复能力，而residual架构仅有部分恢复。消融结果支持架构性解释。预处理一致性地干扰干净预测但对对抗预测影响甚微，这一不对称性可直接测量并用于检测。标准图像质量指标无法可靠代理防御效果。

Conclusion: 预处理防御在边缘主流架构上并非可靠的恢复机制，但可作为有效的检测信号来源；同时需警惕以图像质量指标评估防御有效性的方法学陷阱。作者为此提供了面向对抗弹性的边缘视觉部署决策框架。

Abstract: Preprocessing-based defenses are the standard first-line response to adversarial attacks on edge vision systems, requiring no retraining, no architectural changes, and widely recommended as model-agnostic mitigations. Yet the foundational evaluations of these defenses were conducted on residual or Inception-class architectures, not on the depthwise-separable CNNs that dominate edge deployments. This untested assumption leaves a gap in the security evaluation literature. This paper closes that gap by evaluating six preprocessing defenses against adversarial perturbations across both architecture families. Across all perturbation levels and defenses tested, the two depthwise-separable architectures show consistently poor recovery while the residual architecture shows partial recovery; ablation results are consistent with an architectural rather than parametric explanation, though only three architectures and one attack family are evaluated. Crucially, this failure is not merely a negative result. The same output divergence that disqualifies preprocessing as a recovery mechanism reveals a detection opportunity: preprocessing consistently disrupts clean predictions while leaving adversarial predictions largely unchanged, an asymmetry that is directly measurable without retraining or architectural modification. We further show that standard image quality metrics are unreliable proxies for defense effectiveness, a methodological gap in current evaluation practice. A practitioner decision framework is provided for adversarially resilient edge vision deployment.

</details>


### [32] [BMCTrack-d: Pig re-identification and tracking via back marks in challenging camera settings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03463)
*David Brunner,Maciej Oczak,Marie Bordes,Jean-Loup Rault,Stephan M. Winkler,Viktoria Dorfer*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出了BMCTrack-d，一种基于背部标记和检测的猪个体识别与追踪方法，在侧视摄像机视角下实现了可靠的猪重识别与追踪，相较于两种强基线在高级追踪准确率上分别提升9.11%和1.03%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统猪只监控多在群体层面进行，原因是同一品种的猪外观高度相似，难以实现个体级长期识别与追踪。此外，已有研究几乎都基于俯视摄像头设置（便于追踪），但在实际应用中并非总可行。因此需要一种在侧视、低分辨率、运动剧烈和严重遮挡等困难条件下仍能实现个体级猪监控的方法。

Method: BMCTrack-d采用基于检测的追踪框架，首先利用神经网络背部标记分类器预测被检测猪的身份；随后引入两个后处理阶段提高重识别的长期可靠性：一是时间预测一致性检查，验证当前身份分配是否与近期预测历史一致；二是去重机制，解决每个时间步内冲突的身份分配。该方法明确优先考虑基于外观的准确重识别而非连续追踪。

Result: 在具有挑战性的测试集上，BMCTrack-d在高级追踪准确率（HOTA）上分别超过BoT-SORT-ReID和TrackTrack-ReID基线9.11%和1.03%。

Conclusion: 实验结果表明，基于背部标记的重识别与追踪方法能够在困难环境条件下实现稳健的猪个体级监控，证明了外观优先的重识别策略在个体级牲畜监控场景中的有效性。

Abstract: Automated pig monitoring is essential for assessing their health, behaviour, and welfare. To date, most pig monitoring solutions operate on the group-level, because individual-level monitoring requires reliable long-term identification and tracking of each animal. For domesticated pigs this remains challenging because pigs of the same breed often have highly uniform appearances. Moreover, research on pig monitoring is almost exclusively reported in top-down view camera settings, which considerably ease tracking, but are not always an option in practice. In this work, BMCTrack-d is presented, a novel tracking-by-detection approach that leverages unique back marks to enable robust pig re-identification and tracking in a challenging side-view camera setting, afflicted by rapidly moving pigs, severe occlusions and low resolution. The method first predicts the detected pigs' identities using a neural network-based back mark classifier. To improve re-identification reliability over time, two dedicated post-processing stages are introduced: a temporal prediction consistency check, which validates the identity assignments against the recent prediction history, and deduplication, which resolves conflicting identity assignments in each time step. By explicitly prioritising accurate, appearance-based re-identification over continuous tracking, the proposed approach addresses a key limitation of existing trackers for individual-level monitoring scenarios. On a demanding test set BMCTrack-d outperforms two strong baselines, BoT-SORT-ReID and TrackTrack-ReID, by 9.11% and 1.03%, respectively, in higher-order tracking accuracy. These results demonstrate the effectiveness of back mark-based re-identification and tracking for robust individual-level pig monitoring in challenging settings.

</details>


### [33] [SafeRestore: Detector-Relative Risk Certificates for Selective Industrial Image Restoration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03475)
*Shaoliang Yang,Jun Wang*

Main category: cs.CV

TL;DR: 本文提出SafeRestore框架，将工业检测流水线中的图像恢复建模为在测量显示图、五个恢复候选和一个复查选项之间的选择性动作问题。该方法使用动作特定拟合分数对候选进行排序，在阈值调优数据上选择门控策略，并在不相交的认证样本上用双侧单侧精确二项检验（分别约束正例条件证据丢失率和全部接受样本的过度激活率）验证固定门控。在4,591张公开Carinthia-S图像的回顾性分裂样本研究中，主策略在五次训练重复中仅一次通过检验，而固定双三次插值和降复杂度变体通过频率更高；在保留形态学场景下证据丢失率升至81.1%-90.3%。文章结论强调该工作的贡献是提供可审计的、检测器相对的决策框架，而非宣称自适应路由优于简单策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工业检测流水线通常在检测器作用前先对测量图像进行恢复处理，但恢复可能抑制对检测器有利的缺陷结构或引发干净区域的误激活。因此需要一种框架来系统地决定哪些恢复图像可以自动返回、哪些需要人工复查，以保证可审计性和风险可控。

Method: 将恢复问题形式化为选择性动作问题，包含测量显示图、五个恢复候选与复查选项；用动作特定拟合分数对候选排序；在阈值调优数据上选择门控策略；在不相交的认证样本上用两个单侧精确二项检验（正例条件证据丢失率和全部接受样本的过度激活率）进行验证，保证是在策略固定且认证结果未知前的边际层面，基于图像级i.i.d.工作模型。

Result: 在4,591张公开Carinthia-S图像的回顾性分裂样本研究中，主策略在五次训练重复中仅一次通过认证（通过门控测试覆盖率为12.0%±26.9%，失败计为零）；固定双三次插值和降复杂度变体通过频率更高。在保留形态学场景下证据丢失发生率升至81.1%-90.3%；KolektorSDD数据集既缺乏检测器能力，也不足以提供目标所需的阳性认证图像。

Conclusion: 本文的贡献在于提供一个可审计的、检测器相对的框架，用于决定恢复图像何时可自动返回、何时必须复查，而非声称自适应路由在当前证据上优于简单策略。研究结果强调简单策略（如固定插值）在现有证据中可能更具实际可靠性。

Abstract: Industrial inspection pipelines often restore a measured image before a detector acts on it, yet restoration can suppress detector-supported defect structure or create clean-region activations. We formulate restoration as a selective action problem over the measured display, five restored candidates, and review.
